LP alternation details:
Given current probability a (nonnegative, sum1). M = max c = peak(a). We want find g≥0 with a*g ≤ M * α? Actually if we solve max sum g s.t. a*g ≤ M, then L=Σg. Solving LP:
- Use `scipy.optimize.linprog` with c = -ones(n) (max sum), A_ub = M - A@g >=0? SciPy form A_ub @ x <= b_ub. So A_ub = conv matrix (2n-1 x n) where row k = a_i for i in [max(0,k-n+1), min(n-1,k)] at column j? For variable g_j, constraint k: Σ_j a_{k-j} g_j ≤ M. So A[k,j]=a_{k-j} if 0≤k-j<n else 0. b_ub=M.
- bounds [(0, None)]*n.
- method='highs'. For n=1024 matrix dense 2047x1024 ~2M floats 16MB, okay. Building each iteration O(n^2). Could optimize sparse? SciPy linprog accepts sparse A_ub (csr_matrix). Build sparse with bands. Use scipy.sparse.

But a*g with a as weights, not symmetric self if g. We can choose M maybe current peak * margin (e.g., 1.0, 0.95?) If M=current, optimum L maybe ≈1; need allow slight > to find direction? def build_conv_matrix(a):
    n=len(a); m=2*n-1
    rows, cols, data
    for k in range(m):
       start=max(0, k-n+1); end=min(n-1,k)
       # columns j = k - i where i in [start,end]? If A[k,j]=a_{k-j}
       j_start = max(0, k-(n-1)); j_end = min(n-1,k)
       i_vals = k - np.arange(j_start, j_end+1)
       data.append(a[i_vals]); cols j_start..
    A = csr_matrix((data,(row_repeats,cols)), shape=(m,n))
```

Detailed optimizer pseudo:
```
def optimize_with_softmax(a, budget, rng, init_beta_theta=20.0, final_beta_theta=1000.0, max_iters=None, name=''):
    a = np.asarray(a,float).copy()
    a = np.clip(a,0,None); s=a.sum(); if s<... return a,inf
    a /= s
    n=len(a)
    best=a.copy(); best_score=score_fast(a)
    current=a.copy(); current_score=best_score
    # ensure positive floor
    eps=1e-300? np.finfo(float).tiny? Add 1e-300 may not change sum; use min_positive=1e-300. a = np.maximum(a, 1e-300); normalize.
    beta_theta = init_beta_theta
    step_alpha = 0.2
    no_improve=0
    iter=0
    while time<budget:
       # compute c and score
       c, current_score = conv_and_score(current)
       if current_score < best_score - 1e-12: best=...; no_improve=0; step*=1.1? 
       else: no_improve+=1; if no_improve>100: perturb; no_improve=0; step*=0.5
       # schedule beta
       beta = beta_theta * n  # because c ~1/n
       w = softmax(beta*c)
       grad = grad_from_weights(current,w)
       h = grad - np.mean(grad)
       scale = np.max(np.abs(h))
       if scale < 1e-14: break
       eta = step_alpha / scale
       # try line search alphas
       best_trial_score = current_score
       best_trial_a = current
       for alpha_mult in alpha_candidates: # e.g. [0.5,1,2,4]
           u = -alpha_mult * step_alpha * h / scale  # exponent max ~alpha*step_alpha
           u = np.clip(u, -MAP_EXP, MAP_EXP)
           trial = current * np.exp(u)
           trial_sum = trial.sum()
           if trial_sum <=0: continue
           trial /= trial_sum
           sc = score_fast(trial)
           if sc < best_trial_score - 1e-12:
               best_trial_score=sc; best_trial_a=trial.copy()
       if best_trial_a is not current:
          current=best_trial; current_score=best_trial_score
          # anneal beta theta: increase if no big change? Use iter fraction
       # beta schedule: theta = theta0 + (theta1-theta0)*(iter/total) if total known; or theta *= 1.0001; cap
       beta_theta = min(final, beta_theta * 1.001)
       # adapt step_alpha: if improved, *=1.05 up to 1.0? else *=0.9
       iter++
       if iter % check: print
    return best, best_score
```
But computing score_fast in line search still does FFT. We can avoid multiple by using a single step with alpha adjusted. But line search 3x cost. For n small okay. To limit, choose alpha based on previous success:
- Try alpha = current_alpha. If new_score < old, accept, current_alpha=min(2,current_alpha*1.05). Else try alpha/2, /4. This is up to 3 evaluations.
Better: Use **hard active subgradient** without LSE? We need c anyway. For new a, c computed. We can accept if improved. Good.

Set `alpha` in [0.02,1.0]. Start 0.1. Map exponent max = alpha. Each trial exponent max alpha; if alpha=1, factors [e^-1,e^1]. Good. For line search multipliers [1,0.5,0.25].

Pseudo:
```
alpha=0.15
for it:
  c, old_score = conv_and_score(cur)
  ...
  h=...
  scale=...
  trial_a=None
  for a_try in [alpha, alpha*0.5, alpha*0.25]:
      z = -a_try * h / scale
      z = clip(z,-8,8)
      trial = cur * exp(z); trial/=trial.sum()
      sc=score_fast(trial)
      if sc < best_try: ...
  if best_try improved:
      cur=best_try
      alpha=min(1.0, alpha*1.1)
  else:
      alpha=max(0.02, alpha*0.7)
      if no_improve > 200: random perturb; alpha=0.1
  beta_theta schedule
```

Now, initializations generation:
```
def make_initializations(n, rng, include_parent=False):
    lists=[]
    a = np.ones(n); lists.append(a)
    # random dirichlet alphas
    for alpha in [0.1,0.25,0.5,1.0,2.0,5.0]:
       g=rng.dirichlet(alpha*np.ones(n)); lists.append(g)
    # symmetric random
    for alpha in [0.5,1,2]:
       g=rng.random(n); a=(g+g[::-1])/2; if sum>0 normalize
    # smooth windows
    i=np.arange(n)/(n-1)
    for p in [0.5,1,2,4,8]: a=(1+np.cos(np.pi*i))**p # zero at edges? maybe >0
    for sigma in [0.2,0.3,0.5,0.7,1.0]: a=np.exp(-((i-0.5)**2)/(2*sigma**2))
    for p in [0.5,1,2,4]: a=1/(1+10*(i-0.5)**2**p)
    # edge-enhanced
    for eps in [0.1,0.3,0.5,0.7]: a=1+eps*np.cos(2*np.pi*i)
    # high-frequency: alternating
    a=np.array([1 if j%2==0 else 0.01 for j in range(n)])
    # 0/1 random densities
    for d in [0.5,0.7,0.9]: bits=rng.random(n)<d; a=bits+1e-6
    # parent if length matches, else downsample
```
Need not too many per n to avoid time. For each n, choose maybe 8-16 init, each with limited time.

LP polish implementation details:
```
def lp_polish(a, budget=60.0, max_iters=20):
    a = normalize(a); n=len(a); best=a; best_score=score_fast(a)
    for it in range(max_iters):
       if time > deadline: break
       c, score = conv_and_score(a); if score<best...
       M = score/(2*n) * 1.000001  # peak
       # Optional: use M_target = best_score/(2n) maybe current best peak; if M<current? Use current.
       A = build_conv_matrix(a)
       try:
          res = linprog(-np.ones(n), A_ub=A, b_ub=np.full(2*n-1, M), bounds=[(0,None)]*n, method='highs')
       except: break
       if not res.success: break
       g = res.x; g=np.clip(g,0,None); L=g.sum()
       if L <= 1.0 + 1e-8: break
       g /= L
       # try convex combos
       improved=False
       for t in [0.1,0.3,0.6,1.0]:
           trial=(1-t)*a + t*g; trial=normalize
           sc=score_fast(trial)
           if sc < best_score - 1e-10:
               a=trial; best=trial.copy(); best_score=sc; improved=True; break? choose best t
       if not improved:
           # maybe try pure g? already t=1
           break
    return best,best_score
```
Choosing M: If current score s, peak M=s/(2n). Feasible g. If s is current best, okay. If due LP numerical, use M=max(M, 1e-12). If L huge? Cap? For a with some zeros, L may be >1 but g may have spikes; okay.
- Need t line search: choose t that minimizes score among [0.1..1]. Use best.
- LP with M=current can yield g with sum L but f*g≤M. Is rescaled g sum1. SOTA claims update reduces self-convolution if t small. We'll accept only if improved.
- Could also use M = best_score/(2n) * (1 + epsilon) but same.
- Build A: row k Column j weight a_{k-j}. If a_{k-j}=0, data 0; can skip zeros to save. But floating small positive all nonzero, so n^2 nonzeros. For n=2048 4M? Wait n^2=4M, not 1M? Earlier total row lengths = n^2. Yes for k=0..2n-2, row lengths: 1+2+...+n + (n-1)+...+1 = n^2. For n=1024, 1,048,576. For n=2048, 4,194,304. CSR memory data 4M*8=32MB, indices 4M*4=16MB, plus row pointers, ~50MB. HiGHS may handle but slow. We'll limit LP n≤1024, maybe 2048 if time. For n=4096 16M nonzeros too much. So no LP for >2048.

Building sparse efficiently:
```
def build_A(a):
  n=len(a); m=2*n-1
  row_chars = []
  for k in range(m):
     j0=max(0,k-(n-1)); j1=min(n-1,k)
     length=j1-j0+1
     row_chars.append(np.arange(j0,j1+1))
  # total n*n
  cols=np.concatenate(row_chars)
  rows=np.concatenate([np.full(len, k) for k...])
  data=np.concatenate([a[k-cols_i] ...])
  A=sp.csr_matrix((data,(rows,cols)), shape=(m,n))
```
Data for each row: a[k - cols_i]. Good. Memory rows int64 4M*8 32MB, cols int32? use int64. Could be high but okay for n=1024 (1M -> 16MB rows+8 cols+8 data=32MB). For n=2048 4M ->128MB, maybe too high. Limit LP n<=1024.

Let's implement `convolve_self(a)`:
```
def convolve_self(a):
    n = len(a)
    if n == 1: return np.array([a[0]*a[0]])
    size = 1
    size_bit = (2*n - 1).bit_length()
    size = 1 << size_bit
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, size, n=size) # irfft(..., n=size)
    c = c[:2*n-1]
    # Clean
    c = np.where(c > 0, c, 0.0) # but np.where alloc; use c[c<0]=0? c contiguous.
    if c.size and c[0]<0... c = np.maximum(c,0.0,out=c)
    return c
```
`np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, n=size)` returns length size. Good.

```
def grad_direct(a,w):
  n=len(a); grad=np.zeros(n)
  for i in range(n):
      # sum j=0..n-1 w[i+j]*a[j]
      grad[i]=2*np.dot(a, w[i:i+n])
  return grad
```
This is O(n²) but in Python loop i with np.dot in C; for n=256, 256 dots of length256 ~65k, fine. For n=512, 262k still okay; could use direct up to 512? But line search many. Use FFT >256.

For FFT gradient:
```
def grad_fft(a,w):
  n=len(a); size=1; while size < 3*n-2: size<<=1
  ar = a[::-1]
  fa = np.fft.rfft(ar, size)
  fw = np.fft.rfft(w, size)
  conv = np.fft.irfft(fa*fw, size)
  return 2.0*conv[n-1:2*n-1]
```

Initializations function top-level:
```
def generate_inits(n, rng, parent, limit=8):
    inits=[]
    def add(a): sanitize; if finite and len n; inits.append
    add(np.ones(n))
    if parent is not None: add(resample_sequence(parent,n))
    # random dirichlet
    alphas=[0.2,0.5,1.0,2.0]
    for alpha in alphas: add(rng.dirichlet(alpha*np.ones(n)))
    # symmetric random
    for alpha in [0.5,1.0,2.0]: g=rng.random(n); a=(g+g[::-1])/2; add(a)
    # smooth windows if limit allows
    if n>1:
      x=np.arange(n)/(n-1)
      for p in [1,2,4]: add((1+np.cos(np.pi*x))**p)
      for sigma in [0.2,0.4,0.7]: add(np.exp(-0.5*((x-0.5)/sigma)**2))
      for a in [0.2,0.5,0.8]: add(1 + a*np.cos(2*np.pi*x))
      # alternating sparse: a = 1 if i%q==0 else eps
      for q in [2,3,4]: arr=np.where(np.arange(n)%q==0,1.0,0.01); add(arr)
    # binary random densities
    for d in [0.5,0.8]: bits=rng.random(n)<d; arr=bits+0.01; add(arr)
    # shuffle? maybe
    # truncate to limit, but ensure first (parent/uniform) included
```

def optimize_with_limit(a, time_limit, rng, theta_start=5.0, theta_end=2000.0, name=''):
    a = sanitize normalize; n=len(a)
    c = convolve_self(a); score = score_from_c(c,n,1.0)
    best = a.copy(); best_score=score
    alpha=0.2
    stall=0
    start=time.monotonic(); limit=start+time_limit
    it=0
    # if time_limit <=0 return
    while time.monotonic() < limit:
        it += 1
        cmax = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
        if cmax <= 0: break
        theta = theta_start * (theta_end/theta_start) ** min(1.0, it / max(100.0, time_limit/0.01))? Hmm based on it not time. Use fraction of elapsed? We can use it with expected iterations? Unknown. Simpler: theta = theta_start; while theta*1.05 < theta_end and it%? increase. Maybe use `theta = min(theta_end, theta_start * (1 + it/50.0))` so reaches 2000 after 400? For time chunk 10s, iters maybe 1000; okay. Use `theta = min(theta_end, theta_start + (theta_end-theta_start)*min(1.0, it/500.0))`. But if iteration speed varies, not time. Fine.
        w = weights_from_c(c, theta)
        grad = gradient_self(a,w)
        h = grad - np.mean(grad)
        scale = np.max(np.abs(h))
        if scale < 1e-15:
             stall += 1
             if stall > 20: break or perturb
             continue
        stall=0
        # line search alphas
        best_trial_score = score
        best_trial_a = None
        best_trial_c = None
        # alphas: alpha, alpha*0.5
        for f in (1.0, 0.5):
           au = alpha*f
           z = -au * h / scale
           z = np.clip(z, -8.0, 8.0)
           trial = a * np.exp(z)
           ss = float(np.sum(trial))
           if ss <= 0: continue
           trial /= ss
           # optional ensure finite
           if not np.all(np.isfinite(trial)): continue
           c_trial = convolve_self(trial)
           sc_trial = score_from_c(c_trial,n,1.0)
           if sc_trial < best_trial_score - 1e-12:
               best_trial_score=sc_trial; best_trial_a=trial; best_trial_c=c_trial
        if best_trial_a is not None:
            a=best_trial_a; c=best_trial_c; score=best_trial_score
            alpha = min(1.5, alpha*1.1)
            if score < best_score:
                best_score=score; best=a.copy(); stall=0
            else:
                stall+=1
        else:
            alpha = max(0.02, alpha*0.8)
            stall += 1
            if stall > 100:
                # perturb
                noise=0.2*rng.standard_normal(n)
                trial = a*np.exp(np.clip(noise,-2,2)); normalize; c_trial=conv; sc...
                # accept if not too bad (score < best_score*1.1?) else revert
                if sc_trial < best_score + 0.05*best_score + 1e-9:
                    a=trial; c=c_trial; score=sc_trial; alpha=0.15; stall=0
                elif it % 200 == 0:
                    break
        if it % 1000 ==0 or (it% int(max(1,time_limit/5)) ==0): print
    return best, best_score, a, c, score

def build_conv_matrix_sparse(a):
    n=len(a); m=2*n-1
    col_idx = np.repeat(np.arange(n, dtype=np.int32), n)
    row_off = np.tile(np.arange(n, dtype=np.int32), n)
    rows = col_idx + row_off
    data = np.tile(a.astype(np.float64), n)
    # Filter rows < m? max rows = (n-1)+(n-1)=2n-2 = m-1 yes.
    A = sp.csr_matrix((data, rows.astype(np.int64), col_idx.astype(np.int64)), shape=(m,n))
    return A
```

```
def sanitize_array(p):
   arr=np.asarray(p, dtype=float)
   arr=arr.ravel()

   arr = arr/arr.sum()

Let's refine `optimize_with_limit` for speed and correctness.

```
def optimize_with_limit(a, time_limit, rng, name=''):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s <= 0 or not np.isfinite(s):
       a = np.ones(len(a)); s=1.0
    a = a / s
    n = len(a)
    if n <= 0: return ...
    c = convolve_self(a)
    score = score_from_c(c, n, 1.0)
    best = a.copy(); best_score = score
    alpha = 0.2
    stall = 0
    start = time.monotonic()
    end = start + max(0.0, time_limit)
    it = 0
    while time.monotonic() < end:
       it += 1
       if it % 50 == 0:
           # maybe ensure finite
           pass
       cmax = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
       if cmax <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(cmax):
           break
       frac = min(1.0, (time.monotonic() - start) / max(1e-9, end - start))
       # theta phase
       if frac < 0.25: theta = 3.0 + (10.0-3.0)*(frac/0.25)
       elif frac < 0.6: theta = 10.0 + (100.0-10.0)*((frac-0.25)/0.35)
       else: theta = 100.0 + (3000.0-100.0)*((frac-0.6)/0.4)
       w = weights_from_c(c, theta)
       if w is None: break
       grad = gradient_self(a, w)
       # Due to FFT, grad might contain nan
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
           # fallback direct? or break
           grad = np.nan_to_num(grad, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
       h = grad - float(np.mean(grad))
       scale = float(np.max(np.abs(h)))
       if scale < 1e-15:
           stall += 1
           if stall > 30:
               # perturb
               trial = random_perturb(a, rng, 0.3)
               c_trial = convolve_self(trial)
               sc_trial = score_from_c(c_trial,n,1.0)
               if np.isfinite(sc_trial) and sc_trial < best_score + 0.05*best_score + 1e-9:
                   a=trial; c=c_trial; score=sc_trial
                   if score < best_score:
                       best_score=score; best=a.copy()
                   stall=0; alpha=0.2
               else:
                   break
           continue
       # line search
       best_trial_a = None; best_trial_c=None; best_trial_score=score
       # Try alpha, alpha/2, alpha/4? Use two for speed maybe. If alpha small and no improve, perhaps try larger? We'll use three: [1,0.5,0.25] but each trial cost. For n large, maybe 2. Use if n<=512: alphas = (1,0.5,0.25) else (1,0.5)
       if n <= 512:
           factors = (1.0, 0.5, 0.25)
       else:
           factors = (1.0, 0.5)
       for f in factors:
           if time.monotonic() >= end: break
           au = alpha * f
           if au <= 0: continue
           z = -au * (h / scale)
           z = np.clip(z, -12.0, 12.0)
           trial = a * np.exp(z)
           ss = float(np.sum(trial))
           if ss <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(ss): continue
           trial = trial / ss
           c_trial = convolve_self(trial)
           sc_trial = score_from_c(c_trial, n, 1.0)
           if np.isfinite(sc_trial) and sc_trial < best_trial_score - 1e-13:
                best_trial_score = sc_trial; best_trial_a=trial; best_trial_c=c_trial
       if best_trial_a is not None:
           a=best_trial_a; c=best_trial_c; score=best_trial_score
           if score < best_score:
               best_score=score; best=a.copy(); stall=0
               alpha = min(2.0, alpha*1.15)
           else:
               # current didn't improve best? but score < old score, so best should update because current==best? Unless current worsened by perturbation. Then score may still < old but >best. Keep alpha? 
               alpha = min(2.0, alpha*1.02)
               stall += 1
       else:
           alpha = max(0.02, alpha*0.8)
           stall += 1
           if stall > 80:
               # perturb
               trial = random_perturb(a, rng, min(1.0, 0.2 + 0.02*stall/80))
               c_trial=convolve_self(trial); sc_trial=score_from_c(c_trial,n,1.0)
               if np.isfinite(sc_trial) and sc_trial < best_score + 0.08*best_score + 1e-9:
                   a=trial; c=c_trial; score=sc_trial
                   if score < best_score: best_score=score; best=a.copy()
                   alpha=0.2; stall=0
               else:
                   # reduce perturbation? try one more with smaller? else break
                   if stall > 200: break
                   stall=0
       if it % 1000 == 0 or (time_limit > 60 and it % max(1, int(time_limit/10)) ==0):
           print(f"{name} it={it} score={score:.8f} best={best_score:.8f} alpha={alpha:.3f} theta={theta:.1f}", flush=True)
    return best, best_score, a
```

def random_perturb(a, rng, strength):
   n=len(a)
   z=rng.standard_normal(n)*strength
   z=np.clip(z,-6,6)
   b=a*np.exp(z)
   # maybe add small uniform to avoid zeros
   if n > 1:
       # mix 0.05 with random? 
       u=rng.random(n)
       b = 0.95*b + 0.05*u
   b=np.clip(b,0,None); s=b.sum(); if s<=0: b=np.ones(n); s=n
   return b/s
```

def next_pow2(m):
    if m <=1: return 1
    return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()

`score_from_c`:
```
def score_from_c(c,n,sum_a):
   if n<=0 or sum_a <=1e-300: return inf
   if c.size==0: return inf
   max_c=float(np.max(c))
   if not np.isfinite(max_c) or max_c <0: max_c=0
   return float(2.0*n*max_c/(sum_a*sum_a))
```
For sum=1, ok. If max_c=0 but sum>0 (all zero impossible), score0? Could happen if underflow; but invalid. If sum<0.01 external inf; local not.

`weights_from_c`:
```
cmax=float(np.max(c))
if cmax <= 0: return None
beta=theta/cmax
if not finite or beta < 0: beta=1.0
if beta > 1e8: beta=1e8
w=np.exp(beta*(c-cmax))
wsum=float(np.sum(w))
if wsum <=0 or not finite:
   w=np.zeros_like(c); w[int(np.argmax(c))]=1.0
else: w/=wsum
return w
```
If theta=0? start3. If c has NaN? score finite? We sanitize c. If c contains NaN, cmax NaN. In optimize, check finite. We can in convolve_self clean NaNs? `c = np.nan_to_num(c, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)` maybe. FFT should no NaN. Add.

`fa = np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], size)`; `fw = np.fft.rfft(w, size)`. w length 2n-1 <= size. Conv slice `conv[n-1:2*n-1]`. For n=1, [0:1]. grad=2*conv.

`resample_sequence`:
```
def resample_sequence(a, n):
   a=np.asarray(a,float); if a.size==0 or n<=0: return None
   a=np.clip(a,0,1000); a=np.nan_to_num(a,nan=0,posinf=0,neginf=0)
   s=a.sum(); if s<=0: return None
   a=a/s
   m=a.size
   if m==n: return a
   x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)*(m/(n-1)? )? Need map to [0,m)

`generate_inits` code. Need `limit` parameter. Let's write top-level:
```
def generate_inits(n, rng, reference, limit):
    cand=[]
    cand.append(np.ones(n))
    if reference is not None:
        ref_res = resample_sequence(reference, n)
        if ref_res is not None: cand.append(ref_res)
    if n > 1:
        x = np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
        # Windows
        for p in (1.0,2.0,4.0):
            cand.append((1.0 + np.cos(np.pi*x))**p)
        for sigma in (0.15,0.25,0.4,0.6):
            cand.append(np.exp(-0.5*((x-0.5)/sigma)**2))
        # cosine edge
        for amp in (0.2,0.4,0.6,0.8):
            cand.append(1.0 + amp*np.cos(2.0*np.pi*x))
        # period patterns
        idx=np.arange(n)
        for q in (2,3,4,5):
            arr=np.where(idx % q == 0, 1.0, 0.05).astype(float)
            cand.append(arr)
        # One-sided geometric
        for b in (0.9,0.95,0.99):
            cand.append(b**idx)
        # reverse geometric maybe covered by reversal? add
        for b in (0.95):
            cand.append(b**(n-1-idx))
    # random
    alphas=(0.25,0.5,1.0,2.0)
    if n <= 4096:
       for alpha in alphas: cand.append(rng.dirichlet(alpha*np.ones(n)))
    else:
       for alpha in (0.5,1.0): cand.append(rng.dirichlet(alpha*np.ones(n)))
    # symmetric random
    for _ in range(2):
       g=rng.random(n); a=(g+g[::-1])*0.5; cand.append(np.maximum(a,1e-6))
    # binary random
    for d in (0.5,0.8):
       bits=(rng.random(n)<d).astype(float)+0.05
       cand.append(bits)
    # If reference not None, also jitter reference
    if reference is not None:
       ref_res=resample_sequence(reference,n) # duplicate? avoid? Could jitter.
       if ref_res is not None:
          jitter=ref_res*(1+0.1*rng.standard_normal(n))
          cand.append(np.maximum(jitter,1e-12))
    # process
    inits=[]
    for raw in cand:
       if len(inits) >= limit: break
       a = sanitize_array(raw)
       if a is None or a.size != n: continue
       # Avoid exact duplicate of previous by simple norm? Could skip.
       inits.append(a)
    return inits
```
No nested functions. Duplicates okay. `rng.dirichlet` with n=8192 and alpha small might produce values extremely small/large? okay. To reduce cost for large, only generate up to limit? Now, `sanitize_array` should not alter length. If input raw length n. If reference resample length n.
```
def sanitize_array(a):
    arr=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size==0: return None
    arr=np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    arr=np.clip(arr,0.0,1000.0)
    s=arr.sum()
    if s <= 1e-12 or not np.isfinite(s): return None
    arr=arr/s
    return arr
```

       except: break

       try res=linprog(-np.ones(n), A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=[(0.0,None)]*n, method='highs', options={'disp': False})
       except: break

       g = g / L
       # If g all zeros? no.

`next_pow2`:
```
def next_pow2(m):
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()
```

`convolve_self`:
```
def convolve_self(a):
    n = int(len(a))
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if DIRECT_CONV <=? set global DIRECT_LIMIT=256
    if n <= DIRECT_LIMIT:
        c = np.convolve(a, a)
    else:
        size = next_pow2(2*n - 1)
        fa = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
        c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, n=size)
        c = c[:2*n - 1]
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    c = np.nan_to_num(c, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    c[c < 0.0] = 0.0
    return c
```
Define `DIRECT_LIMIT = 256` top-level constant (not closure). Good.

`score_fast`:
```
def score_fast(a):
    arr=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    if arr.size==0: return np.inf
    s=float(np.sum(arr))
    if s <= 0 or not np.isfinite(s): return np.inf
    c=convolve_self(arr)
    return score_from_c(c, arr.size, s)
```
But for optimization we often have normalized a and c already; use `score_from_c`. `score_fast` for initial scoring and LP.

`random_perturb`:
```
def random_perturb(a, rng, strength):
    n=len(a)
    if n==0: return a
    z = rng.standard_normal(n) * float(strength)
    z = np.clip(z, -6.0, 6.0)
    b = a * np.exp(z)
    # add a little random floor
    if n > 1:
       u = rng.random(n)
       b = 0.98*b + 0.02*u
    b = np.clip(b, 0.0, None)
    s=float(np.sum(b))
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
       return np.ones(n)/n
    return b/s
```
This may move toward uniform (adding random) but okay.

Potential issue: If a has zeros, `a*np.exp(z)` zeros remain zero; adding 0.02*u makes all positive. Good. But mixture 0.98/0.02 may destroy sparsity too much. Use 0.01. okay.

Now, `build_conv_matrix_sparse`: Use `csr_matrix`. Ensure import.
```
def build_conv_matrix_sparse(a):
    n=len(a); m=2*n-1
    col_idx = np.repeat(np.arange(n, dtype=np.int32), n)
    row_off = np.tile(np.arange(n, dtype=np.int32), n)
    rows = (col_idx + row_off).astype(np.int64)
    data = np.tile(np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64), n)
    # Filter out any data <=0? If a has zeros, but all close. To reduce, we could mask = data>0; but creating mask huge. If a sparse, maybe helpful. We can do if n<=512? not.
    return csr_matrix((data, rows, col_idx.astype(np.int64)), shape=(m,n))
```
Memory: `np.tile` duplicates a; data contiguous. `rows` and `col_idx` int64 conversion after addition? `col_idx+row_off` int32 may overflow for n>46340? n≤1024 no. But cast to int64.
Could avoid storing col_idx int64 by using int32? csr may accept int32. Use int64 for safety. Memory ok.

`lp_polish`: Because `score_fast` recomputes c; we can use current score but after update compute. Good.
```
def lp_polish(a, time_limit, name=''):
    a=sanitize_array(a); if None return None,inf
    n=len(a); if n<2 or n>1100 or not SCIPY_OK: return a, score_fast(a)
    start=time.monotonic(); end=start+time_limit
    current=a.copy(); c=convolve_self(current); current_score=score_from_c(c,n,1.0)
    best=current.copy(); best_score=current_score
    max_iters = 25 if n<=256 else (12 if n<=512 else 6)
    scale=1.0e6
    for it in range(max_iters):
       if time.monotonic() >= end: break
       M = current_score/(2.0*n)
       if not finite or M<=0: break
       M *= 1.000001
       try: A=build_conv_matrix_sparse(current)
       except Exception: break
       b=np.full(2*n-1, M*scale, dtype=np.float64)
       c_obj=np.ones(n); c_obj *= -1.0
       bounds=[(0.0, None)]*n
       try:
          res=linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=bounds, method='highs', options={'disp': False})
       except Exception:
          break
       if res is None or not getattr(res,'success',False): break
       g=np.asarray(res.x,dtype=np.float64)
       g=np.nan_to_num(g,nan=0.0,posinf=0.0,neginf=0.0)
       g=np.clip(g,0.0,None)
       L=float(np.sum(g))
       if L <= scale*(1.0+1e-5): break
       g = g / L
       best_t=None; best_t_score=current_score; best_t_a=None
       for t in (0.05,0.1,0.25,0.5,1.0):
          if time.monotonic() >= end: break
          trial=(1.0-t)*current + t*g
          trial=sanitize_array(trial)
          if trial is None: continue
          sc=score_fast(trial)
          if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_t_score - 1e-13:
             best_t_score=sc; best_t_a=trial
       if best_t_a is None:
          break
       current=best_t_a; current_score=best_t_score
       if current_score < best_score:
          best_score=current_score; best=current.copy()
    return best,best_score
```
If LP repeatedly fails, okay.

Potential bug: `c_obj=np.ones(n); c_obj *= -1.0` top-level no issue.

def optimize_length(n, budget, rng, reference, global_best):
    if n <=0 or budget <=1: return None,np.inf
    initial_limit = init_limit_for_n(n)
    inits = generate_inits(n, rng, reference, initial_limit)
    scored=[]
    for idx_init, a in enumerate(inits):
        if time left? not here; assume no global deadline passed. Could pass deadline.
        a=sanitize_array(a)
        if a is None: continue
        c=convolve_self(a)
        sc=score_from_c(c,n,1.0)
        if np.isfinite(sc): scored.append((sc, idx_init, a))
    if not scored:
        a=np.ones(n)/n; return a, score_fast(a)
    scored.sort()
    # Keep based on budget
    if budget > 600: keep= min(len(scored),4)
    elif budget > 200: keep= min(len(scored),3)
    elif budget > 60: keep= min(len(scored),2)
    else: keep=1
    selected=[]
    for item in scored[:keep]: selected.append(item[2])
    # weight distribution
    weights=[1.0]*keep
    if keep>=4: weights=[0.45,0.30,0.17,0.08]
    elif keep==3: weights=[0.55,0.30,0.15]
    elif keep==2: weights=[0.65,0.35]
    else: weights=[1.0]
    # LP budget reserve
    if n <= 1100 and budget > 120:
        lp_budget = min(180.0, 0.2*budget)
        grad_budget = max(5.0, budget - lp_budget)
    else:
        lp_budget=0.0; grad_budget=budget
    best_a=None; best_sc=np.inf
    for k in range(keep):
       t_alloc = grad_budget * weights[k]
       # reduce if remaining? We don't have deadline here. Need pass deadline to optimize_length.
```

Main `propose_candidate`:
```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400):
    start=time.monotonic()
    try: total=max(0.0,float(budget_s))
    except: total=2400
    if total <=10: return [1.0,1.0]? maybe uniform 2
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    parent = None
    if 'height_sequence_1' in globals():
        p=globals()['height_sequence_1']
        if p is not None:
            arr=sanitize_array(p)
            if arr is not None: parent=arr
    anchor = 256
    if parent is not None:
        p_n=len(parent)
        if 8 <= p_n <= 8192: anchor=p_n
    # build n_set
    base=[16,32,64,128,256,512,1024,2048,4096,8192]
    extra=[anchor, max(8,anchor//2), min(8192,anchor*2)]
    n_set=set(base)
    for x in extra:
       if 8<=x<=8192: n_set.add(int(x))
    # If parent length not power, include; may be many. If >12, trim by weights later? We'll keep up to 10.

Detailed `generate_inits`:
```
def generate_inits(n, rng, reference, limit):
    cand=[]
    cand.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))
    if reference is not None:
        r1 = resample_sequence(reference, n)
        if r1 is not None:
            cand.append(r1)
            rj = resample_sequence(reference, n)
            if rj is not None:
                jitter = rj * (1.0 + 0.15 * rng.standard_normal(n))
                cand.append(np.maximum(jitter, 1e-12))
    # one random dirichlet early
    if n <= 8192:
        cand.append(rng.dirichlet(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)))
    if n <= 4096:
        cand.append(rng.dirichlet(0.5 * np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)))
    # structured if n>1
    if n > 1:
       x = np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
       for p in (2.0,1.0,4.0): cand.append((1.0+np.cos(np.pi*x))**p)
       for sigma in (0.25,0.4,0.15,0.6): cand.append(np.exp(-0.5*((x-0.5)/sigma)**2))
       for amp in (0.5,0.3,0.7,0.8): cand.append(1.0+amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*x))
       idx=np.arange(n)
       for q in (3,4,5,2): arr=np.where(idx % q ==0, 1.0, 0.05); cand.append(arr.astype(np.float64))
       for b in (0.95,0.9): cand.append(b ** idx.astype(np.float64))
    # random symmetric
    for _ in range(2):
       g=rng.random(n); a=(g+g[::-1])*0.5; cand.append(np.maximum(a,1e-6))
    # binary random
    for d in (0.7,0.5):
       bits=(rng.random(n)<d).astype(np.float64) + 0.05
       cand.append(bits)
    inits=[]
    for raw in cand:
       if len(inits) >= limit: break
       a=sanitize_array(raw)
       if a is None or a.size != n: continue
       inits.append(a)
    return inits
```
`np.where` returns float array because values float. `b ** idx.astype(float)` for b=0.9 idx large may underflow but okay. For b=0.9 and n=8192, last 0, sum finite. Good.
If n=0? propose doesn't call n<8, but safe. `np.ones(0)` size0 sanitize None; return empty.

```
def optimize_length(n, budget, rng, reference, deadline):
    if n <= 0 or budget <= 0: return None, np.inf
    inits=generate_inits(n, rng, reference, init_limit_for_n(n))
    scored=[]
    for k, raw in enumerate(inits):
        a=sanitize_array(raw)
        if a is None: continue
        c=convolve_self(a)
        sc=score_from_c(c,n,1.0)
        if np.isfinite(sc): scored.append((sc,k,a))
    if not scored:
        a=np.ones(n)/n
        sc=score_fast(a)
        return a, sc
    scored.sort()
    if budget > 800.0: keep=min(len(scored),4)
    elif budget > 300.0: keep=min(len(scored),3)
    elif budget > 100.0: keep=min(scored,2)? typo
    else: keep=1
    selected=[scored[i][2] for i in range(keep)]
    if keep == 1: weights=[1.0]
    elif keep == 2: weights=[0.65,0.35]
    elif keep == 3: weights=[0.55,0.30,0.15]
    else: weights=[0.45,0.30,0.17,0.08]
    lp_budget=0.0; grad_budget=budget
    if n <= LP_N_LIMIT and budget > 150.0:
        lp_budget=min(240.0, 0.2*budget)
        grad_budget=max(10.0, budget-lp_budget)
    best_a=None; best_sc=np.inf
    for k in range(keep):
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline: break
        rem=deadline-time.monotonic()
        if rem < 5: break
        t_use=min(grad_budget*weights[k], rem-5.0)
        if t_use <=1: continue
        a_best, sc_best, _ = optimize_with_limit(selected[k], t_use, rng, f"n{n}_i{k}")
        if np.isfinite(sc_best) and sc_best < best_sc:
             best_sc=sc_best; best_a=a_best
    if best_a is None and len(scored)>0:
        best_sc=scored[0][0]; best_a=scored[0][2]
    if best_a is not None and n <= LP_N_LIMIT and lp_budget > 20:
        if time.monotonic() < deadline - 5:
            lp_time = min(lp_budget, max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 10))
            a_lp, sc_lp = lp_polish(best_a, lp_time, f"lp_n{n}")
            if np.isfinite(sc_lp) and sc_lp < best_sc:
                best_sc=sc_lp; best_a=a_lp
            # If time remains, maybe lp second candidate
            if len(selected)>1 and lp_time * 2 < deadline - time.monotonic() - 10:
                 a(lp? )
    return best_a, best_sc

def try_evaluate_sequence(seq_list):
    try:
        val = evaluate_sequence(seq_list)

            return float(val)
    except Exception:

def array_to_valid_list(arr):
    a=np.asarray(arr,dtype=np.float64)
    a=np.nan_to_num(a,nan=0.0,posinf=1000.0,neginf=0.0)

    if s > 0:
        a = a / s
    return [float(x) for x in a.tolist()]

```python
"""
Search algorithm for minimizing
    2*n*max(convolve(a,a))/sum(a)^2
over non-negative sequences.

The objective is homogeneous, so the search keeps sequences normalized to
sum 1.  A multiplicative natural-gradient method is used on a smoothed
log-sum-exp approximation of the peak convolution coefficient, with annealed
sharpness and adaptive steps.  Several structured/random initializations are
tried for several lengths, using the best previous construction as a
resampling reference.  For moderate lengths, a final polish is performed with
the LP alternation from the previous state-of-the-art idea:
maximize sum(g) subject to a*g <= M, then move toward the rescaled g if it
reduces the peak self-convolution.  The best local candidate is finally
confirmed by the provided evaluate_sequence().
"""

import time
import numpy as np

try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
    from scipy.sparse import csr_matrix
    SCIPY_OK = True
except Exception:
    SCIPY_OK = False

DIRECT_LIMIT = 256
LP_N_LIMIT = 1000
MAP_EXP = 12.0


def next_pow2(m):
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def sanitize_array(a):
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        return None
    arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(np.sum(arr))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12:
        return None
    arr = arr / s
    return arr


def resample_sequence(a, n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if n <= 0 or a.size == 0:
        return None
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12:
        return None
    a = a / s
    m = a.size
    if m == n:
        return a.copy()
    if m == 1:
        out = np.full(n, float(a[0]), dtype=np.float64)
        return out
    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) * (float(m) / float(n))
    out = np.interp(x, np.arange(m, dtype=np.float64), a)
    out = np.clip(out, 0.0, None)
    s = float(np.sum(out))
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return None
    return out / s


def convolve_self(a):
    n = int(len(a))
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if n <= DIRECT_LIMIT:
        c = np.convolve(a, a)
    else:
        size = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
        fa = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
        c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, n=size)
        c = c[:2 * n - 1]
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    c = np.nan_to_num(c, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    c[c < 0.0] = 0.0
    return c


def score_from_c(c, n, sum_a):
    if n <= 0:
        return np.inf
    if sum_a <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(sum_a):
        return np.inf
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    max_c = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(max_c):
        return np.inf
    if max_c < 0.0:
        max_c = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * n * max_c / (sum_a * sum_a))


def score_fast(a):
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if arr.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    s = float(np.sum(arr))
    if s <= 1e-12 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.inf
    c = convolve_self(arr)
    return score_from_c(c, arr.size, s)


def weights_from_c(c, theta):
    if c.size == 0:
        return None
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(cmax) or cmax <= 1e-300:
        return None
    beta = theta / cmax
    if not np.isfinite(beta) or beta <= 0.0:
        beta = 1.0
    if beta > 1.0e8:
        beta = 1.0e8
    w = np.exp(beta * (c - cmax))
    wsum = float(np.sum(w))
    if not np.isfinite(wsum) or wsum <= 1e-300:
        w = np.zeros_like(c)
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        return w
    return w / wsum


def gradient_self(a, w):
    n = len(a)
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([2.0 * a[0] * w[0]], dtype=np.float64)
    if n <= DIRECT_LIMIT:
        grad = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for i in range(n):
            grad[i] = 2.0 * np.dot(a, w[i:i + n])
        grad = np.nan_to_num(grad, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        grad[grad < 0.0] = 0.0
        return grad
    size = next_pow2(3 * n - 2)
    ar = a[::-1]
    fa = np.fft.rfft(ar, size)
    fw = np.fft.rfft(w, size)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(fa * fw, n=size)
    grad = 2.0 * conv[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
    grad = np.asarray(grad, dtype=np.float64)
    grad = np.nan_to_num(grad, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    grad[grad < 0.0] = 0.0
    return grad


def random_perturb(a, rng, strength):
    n = len(a)
    if n == 0:
        return a
    z = rng.standard_normal(n) * float(strength)
    z = np.clip(z, -6.0, 6.0)
    b = a * np.exp(z)
    if n > 1:
        u = rng.random(n)
        b = 0.98 * b + 0.02 * u
    b = np.clip(b, 0.0, None)
    s = float(np.sum(b))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return b / s


def optimize_with_limit(a, time_limit, rng, name=""):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if a.size == 0:
        a = np.ones(8, dtype=np.float64) / 8.0
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12:
        a = np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(a.size)
        s = 1.0
    a = a / s
    n = a.size

    if time_limit is None or time_limit <= 0.0:
        c = convolve_self(a)
        sc = score_from_c(c, n, 1.0)
        return a, sc, a

    c = convolve_self(a)
    score = score_from_c(c, n, 1.0)
    best = a.copy()
    best_score = score
    alpha = 0.20
    stall = 0
    start = time.monotonic()
    end = start + float(time_limit)
    run_time = max(1e-9, end - start)
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < end:
        it += 1
        cmax = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
        if not np.isfinite(cmax) or cmax <= 1e-300:
            break

        frac = min(1.0, (time.monotonic() - start) / run_time)
        if frac < 0.25:
            theta = 3.0 + (10.0 - 3.0) * (frac / 0.25)
        elif frac < 0.60:
            theta = 10.0 + (100.0 - 10.0) * ((frac - 0.25) / 0.35)
        else:
            theta = 100.0 + (1000.0 - 100.0) * ((frac - 0.60) / 0.40)

        w = weights_from_c(c, theta)
        if w is None:
            break

        grad = gradient_self(a, w)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
            grad = np.nan_to_num(grad, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)

        h = grad - float(np.mean(grad))
        scale = float(np.max(np.abs(h)))
        if scale < 1e-15:
            stall += 1
            if stall > 30:
                trial = random_perturb(a, rng, 0.35)
                tc = convolve_self(trial)
                tsc = score_from_c(tc, n, 1.0)
                if np.isfinite(tsc) and tsc < best_score + 0.05 * best_score + 1e-9:
                    a = trial
                    c = tc
                    score = tsc
                    if score < best_score:
                        best = a.copy()
                        best_score = score
                    alpha = 0.20
                    stall = 0
                else:
                    break
            continue

        best_trial_a = None
        best_trial_c = None
        best_trial_score = score

        if n <= 512:
            factors = (1.0, 0.5, 0.25)
        else:
            factors = (1.0, 0.5)

        for f in factors:
            if time.monotonic() >= end:
                break
            au = alpha * f
            if au <= 0.0:
                continue
            z = -au * (h / scale)
            z = np.clip(z, -MAP_EXP, MAP_EXP)
            trial = a * np.exp(z)
            ss = float(np.sum(trial))
            if not np.isfinite(ss) or ss <= 1e-300:
                continue
            trial = trial / ss
            tc = convolve_self(trial)
            tsc = score_from_c(tc, n, 1.0)
            if np.isfinite(tsc) and tsc < best_trial_score - 1e-13:
                best_trial_score = tsc
                best_trial_a = trial
                best_trial_c = tc

        if best_trial_a is not None:
            a = best_trial_a
            c = best_trial_c
            score = best_trial_score
            if score < best_score:
                best_score = score
                best = a.copy()
                stall = 0
                alpha = min(2.0, alpha * 1.15)
            else:
                alpha = min(2.0, alpha * 1.02)
                stall += 1
        else:
            alpha = max(0.02, alpha * 0.80)
            stall += 1
            if stall > 80:
                trial = random_perturb(a, rng, min(1.0, 0.20 + 0.02 * stall / 80.0))
                tc = convolve_self(trial)
                tsc = score_from_c(tc, n, 1.0)
                if np.isfinite(tsc) and tsc < best_score + 0.08 * best_score + 1e-9:
                    a = trial
                    c = tc
                    score = tsc
                    if score < best_score:
                        best_score = score
                        best = a.copy()
                    alpha = 0.20
                    stall = 0
                elif stall > 240:
                    break
                else:
                    stall = 0

        if it % 1000 == 0 or (float(time_limit) > 90.0 and it % max(1, int(time_limit / 12)) == 0):
            print("    %s it=%d score=%.8f best=%.8f alpha=%.3f theta=%.1f"
                  % (name, it, score, best_score, alpha, theta), flush=True)

    return best, best_score, a


def init_limit_for_n(n):
    if n <= 128:
        return 12
    if n <= 1024:
        return 10
    if n <= 4096:
        return 8
    return 6


def generate_inits(n, rng, reference, limit):
    cand = []
    cand.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))

    if reference is not None:
        r1 = resample_sequence(reference, n)
        if r1 is not None:
            cand.append(r1)
            r2 = resample_sequence(reference, n)
            if r2 is not None:
                jitter = r2 * (1.0 + 0.15 * rng.standard_normal(n))
                cand.append(np.maximum(jitter, 1e-12))

    if n <= 8192:
        cand.append(rng.dirichlet(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)))
    if n <= 4096:
        cand.append(rng.dirichlet(0.5 * np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)))

    if n > 1:
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)

        for p in (2.0, 1.0, 4.0):
            cand.append((1.0 + np.cos(np.pi * x)) ** p)

        for sigma in (0.25, 0.40, 0.15, 0.60):
            cand.append(np.exp(-0.5 * ((x - 0.5) / sigma) ** 2))

        for amp in (0.5, 0.3, 0.7, 0.8):
            cand.append(1.0 + amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x))

        idx = np.arange(n)
        for q in (3, 4, 5, 2):
            arr = np.where(idx % q == 0, 1.0, 0.05).astype(np.float64)
            cand.append(arr)

        for b in (0.95, 0.90):
            cand.append(b ** idx.astype(np.float64))

    for _ in range(2):
        g = rng.random(n)
        a = (g + g[::-1]) * 0.5
        cand.append(np.maximum(a, 1e-6))

    for d in (0.7, 0.5):
        bits = (rng.random(n) < d).astype(np.float64) + 0.05
        cand.append(bits)

    inits = []
    for raw in cand:
        if len(inits) >= limit:
            break
        a = sanitize_array(raw)
        if a is None or a.size != n:
            continue
        inits.append(a)
    return inits


def build_conv_matrix_sparse(a):
    n = len(a)
    m = 2 * n - 1
    col_idx = np.repeat(np.arange(n, dtype=np.int32), n)
    row_off = np.tile(np.arange(n, dtype=np.int32), n)
    rows = (col_idx + row_off).astype(np.int64)
    cols = col_idx.astype(np.int64)
    data = np.tile(np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64), n)
    return csr_matrix((data, rows, cols), shape=(m, n))


def lp_polish(a, time_limit, name=""):
    a = sanitize_array(a)
    if a is None:
        return None, np.inf
    n = len(a)
    if n < 2 or n > LP_N_LIMIT or not SCIPY_OK:
        return a.copy(), score_fast(a)
    if time_limit is None or time_limit <= 0.0:
        return a.copy(), score_fast(a)

    start = time.monotonic()
    end = start + float(time_limit)
    current = a.copy()
    current_score = score_fast(current)
    best = current.copy()
    best_score = current_score

    if n <= 256:
        max_iters = 25
    elif n <= 512:
        max_iters = 12
    elif n <= 800:
        max_iters = 7
    else:
        max_iters = 4

    scale = 1.0e6
    for _ in range(max_iters):
        if time.monotonic() >= end:
            break
        M = current_score / (2.0 * float(n))
        if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
            break
        M *= 1.0 + 1e-6

        try:
            A = build_conv_matrix_sparse(current)
        except Exception:
            break

        b = np.full(2 * n - 1, M * scale, dtype=np.float64)
        c_obj = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        bounds = [(0.0, None)] * n

        try:
            res = linprog(
                c_obj,
                A_ub=A,
                b_ub=b,
                bounds=bounds,
                method="highs",
                options={"disp": False}
            )
        except Exception:
            break

        if res is None or not getattr(res, "success", False):
            break

        g = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
        g = np.nan_to_num(g, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        g = np.clip(g, 0.0, None)
        L = float(np.sum(g))
        if not np.isfinite(L) or L <= scale * (1.0 + 1e-5):
            break
        g = g / L

        best_t_score = current_score
        best_t_a = None
        for t in (0.05, 0.10, 0.25, 0.50, 1.00):
            if time.monotonic() >= end:
                break
            trial = (1.0 - t) * current + t * g
            trial = sanitize_array(trial)
            if trial is None:
                continue
            sc = score_fast(trial)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_t_score - 1e-13:
                best_t_score = sc
                best_t_a = trial

        if best_t_a is None:
            break

        current = best_t_a
        current_score = best_t_score
        if current_score < best_score:
            best_score = current_score
            best = current.copy()

    return best, best_score


def array_to_valid_list(arr):
    a = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.01:
        a = np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64)
        s = 256.0
    if s > 0.0:
        a = a / s
    return [float(x) for x in a.tolist()]


def try_evaluate_sequence(seq_list):
    try:
        val = evaluate_sequence(seq_list)
        if np.isfinite(val):
            return float(val)
    except Exception:
        pass
    return np.inf


def optimize_length(n, budget, rng, reference, deadline):
    if n <= 0 or budget <= 0.0:
        return None, np.inf

    inits = generate_inits(n, rng, reference, init_limit_for_n(n))
    scored = []
    for k, raw in enumerate(inits):
        a = sanitize_array(raw)
        if a is None:
            continue
        c = convolve_self(a)
        sc = score_from_c(c, n, 1.0)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            scored.append((sc, k, a))

    if not scored:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        return a, score_fast(a)

    scored.sort()

    if budget > 800.0:
        keep = min(len(scored), 4)
    elif budget > 300.0:
        keep = min(len(scored), 3)
    elif budget > 100.0:
        keep = min(len(scored), 2)
    else:
        keep = 1

    selected = [scored[i][2] for i in range(keep)]

    if keep == 1:
        weights = [1.0]
    elif keep == 2:
        weights = [0.65, 0.35]
    elif keep == 3:
        weights = [0.55, 0.30, 0.15]
    else:
        weights = [0.45, 0.30, 0.17, 0.08]

    lp_budget = 0.0
    grad_budget = float(budget)
    if n <= LP_N_LIMIT and budget > 150.0:
        lp_budget = min(240.0, 0.20 * budget)
        grad_budget = max(10.0, budget - lp_budget)

    best_a = None
    best_sc = np.inf

    for k in range(keep):
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 5.0:
            break
        t_use = min(grad_budget * weights[k], rem - 5.0)
        if t_use <= 1.0:
            continue
        a_best, sc_best, _ = optimize_with_limit(selected[k], t_use, rng, "n%d_i%d" % (n, k))
        if np.isfinite(sc_best) and sc_best < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc_best
            best_a = a_best

    if best_a is None and len(scored) > 0:
        best_sc = float(scored[0][0])
        best_a = scored[0][2]

    if best_a is not None and n <= LP_N_LIMIT and lp_budget > 20.0:
        if time.monotonic() < deadline - 5.0:
            lp_time = min(lp_budget, max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 10.0))
            a_lp, sc_lp = lp_polish(best_a, lp_time, "lp_n%d" % n)
            if np.isfinite(sc_lp) and sc_lp < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc_lp
                best_a = a_lp

    return best_a, best_sc


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    try:
        total_budget = max(0.0, float(budget_s))
    except Exception:
        total_budget = 2400.0

    if total_budget <= 5.0:
        return array_to_valid_list(np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64))

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    parent = None
    parent_n = 0
    try:
        if "height_sequence_1" in globals():
            p = globals()["height_sequence_1"]
            if p is not None:
                parent = sanitize_array(p)
                if parent is not None:
                    parent_n = len(parent)
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    anchor = 256
    if parent is not None and 8 <= parent_n <= 8192:
        anchor = parent_n

    base_n = [16, 32, 64, 128, 256, 512, 1024, 2048, 4096, 8192]
    n_set = set(base_n)
    if parent is not None and 8 <= parent_n <= 8192:
        n_set.add(parent_n)
        n_set.add(max(8, parent_n // 2))
        n_set.add(min(8192, parent_n * 2))
    n_set.add(max(8, anchor // 2))
    n_set.add(min(8192, anchor * 2))
    n_set = set(x for x in n_set if 8 <= x <= 8192)

    if len(n_set) > 10:
        keep = set([anchor, 128, 256, 512, 1024, 2048])
        if parent is not None:
            keep.add(parent_n)
        n_set = set(x for x in n_set if x in keep)

    n_list = sorted(n_set)
    search_budget = max(0.0, total_budget - 60.0)

    raw_weights = {}
    for n in n_list:
        if n < 64:
            w = 0.25
        elif n < 128:
            w = 0.5
        elif n <= 512:
            w = 2.0
        elif n <= 1024:
            w = 2.0
        elif n <= 2048:
            w = 1.2
        else:
            w = 0.7
        if parent is not None and n == parent_n:
            w *= 3.0
        if n == anchor:
            w *= 1.5
        raw_weights[n] = float(w)

    sum_w = float(sum(raw_weights.values()))
    if sum_w <= 0.0:
        raw_weights = {anchor: 1.0}
        sum_w = 1.0

    budgets = {}
    for n, w in raw_weights.items():
        budgets[n] = search_budget * (w / sum_w)

    order = []
    if anchor in budgets:
        order.append(anchor)
    rest = []
    anchor_f = float(max(1, anchor))
    for n in n_list:
        if n == anchor:
            continue
        key = abs(float(np.log(float(max(1, n)) / anchor_f)))
        rest.append((key, n))
    rest.sort()
    for _, n in rest:
        order.append(n)

    deadline = t0 + max(0.0, total_budget - 30.0)

    pool = []
    best_global_score = np.inf
    best_global_a = None

    uniform = np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64)
    sc = score_fast(uniform)
    pool.append((sc, 0, uniform))
    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_global_score:
        best_global_score = sc
        best_global_a = uniform

    if parent is not None:
        scp = score_fast(parent)
        pool.append((scp, len(pool), parent))
        if np.isfinite(scp) and scp < best_global_score:
            best_global_score = scp
            best_global_a = parent
        print("parent sequence: n=%d local_score=%.8f" % (len(parent), scp), flush=True)

    reference = best_global_a

    for n in order:
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break
        b = budgets.get(n, 0.0)
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 10.0:
            break
        b_use = min(float(b), rem - 10.0)
        if b_use <= 5.0:
            continue
        print("n=%d allocated=%.1fs current_best=%.8f" % (n, b_use, best_global_score), flush=True)
        a_n, sc_n = optimize_length(n, b_use, rng, reference, deadline)
        if a_n is not None and np.isfinite(sc_n):
            pool.append((sc_n, len(pool), a_n))
            if sc_n < best_global_score:
                best_global_score = sc_n
                best_global_a = a_n
                reference = a_n
            print("  n=%d improved to %.8f" % (n, sc_n), flush=True)

    if best_global_a is None:
        best_global_a = np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64)
        best_global_score = 2.0

    pool.sort()

    final_list = None
    final_oracle = np.inf
    evals = 0
    n_big_evals = 0

    for item in pool:
        if evals >= 14:
            break
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break
        local_score, _, a = item
        if not np.isfinite(local_score):
            continue
        seq = array_to_valid_list(a)
        nlen = len(seq)
        if nlen > 4096:
            if n_big_evals >= 3:
                continue
            n_big_evals += 1
        o = try_evaluate_sequence(seq)
        evals += 1
        print("final candidate: n=%d local=%.8f oracle=%.8f" % (nlen, local_score, o), flush=True)
        if np.isfinite(o) and o < final_oracle:
            final_oracle = float(o)
            final_list = seq

    if final_list is None:
        final_list = array_to_valid_list(best_global_a)
        o = try_evaluate_sequence(final_list)
        if np.isfinite(o):
            final_oracle = float(o)
            print("fallback oracle score=%.8f n=%d" % (final_oracle, len(final_list)), flush=True)
        else:
            print("fallback local score=%.8f n=%d" % (
                float(best_global_score), len(final_list)), flush=True)
    else:
        print("FINAL selected oracle=%.8f n=%d" % (final_oracle, len(final_list)), flush=True)

    return final_list
```
